#### Exhibit Program Management
*Tags: exhibit program, trade show, cost management, asset management · Source: https://www.czarco.com/studio/czarnowski*

Czarnowski offers an Exhibit Program Playbook designed to help brands move beyond one-off trade show builds and into a managed exhibit program. The playbook addresses cost-per-show analysis, right-sizing each build for the appropriate show, and ensuring every warehoused asset is earning its return. The company also offers a four-question exhibit program test to help brands identify whether their current approach constitutes a real program or a collection of uncoordinated individual decisions.

#### Brand strategy and planning approach
*Tags: brand strategy, planning, process, positioning · Source: https://www.czarco.com/studio/public-school*

Public School's brand strategy and planning work is centered on uncovering what makes a client's brand matter and then building a clear roadmap to prove it. This forms the strategic foundation from which all other creative and campaign work is developed.

#### Integrated Sports Execution Model
*Tags: integrated execution, end-to-end, process, on-site operations · Source: https://www.czarco.com/industry/sports*

Czarnowski Collective operates as a single integrated team across every phase of a sports activation: strategy, design, engineering, fabrication, install, digital integration, and on-site operations. This end-to-end ownership model eliminates handoff chaos and keeps accountability in one place from concept through execution.

#### Health and Life Science Design Protocol
*Tags: process, health, compliance, design · Source: https://www.czarco.com/industry/health-and-life-science*

Czarnowski Collective follows a structured protocol for health and life science engagements. The process begins with a deep dive into the science and clinical narrative, covering everything from mechanism of action to portfolio positioning, grounded in substance rather than surface-level aesthetics. Design is then executed within established regulatory guardrails, with compliance treated as a design parameter from the outset. Collaboration with clients is ongoing throughout the engagement, with alignment treated as a methodology rather than a single meeting. Finally, every experience is built to balance evidence with empathy, using data to inform and story to connect with the target audience.

#### Approach to Complex Technical Storytelling
*Tags: process, storytelling, technical, manufacturing · Source: https://www.czarco.com/industry/manufacturing*

Czarnowski Collective follows a disciplined blueprint for manufacturing clients: study the system before shaping the narrative, surface the real differentiators rather than the marketing version, translate complexity into clarity without talking down to anyone, and build durable solutions designed to last. The agency does not paraphrase what it does not understand, and it does not rely on breathless superlatives. The result is credible, well-built work designed to hold up under the scrutiny of technical and industrial audiences.

#### Aerospace and Defense Engagement Process (The Flight Plan)
*Tags: process, aerospace, defense, execution · Source: https://www.czarco.com/industry/aerospace*

Czarnowski Collective follows a structured engagement approach for aerospace and defense programs called The Flight Plan. The process begins by aligning with the client's technical objectives before any creative or production work begins. Design is then executed with credibility at every detail, because the audience notices everything. Logistics planning accounts for complex environments and secure requirements. Execution is held to zero-compromise standards throughout.

#### Consumer Brand Experience Design Process
*Tags: consumer brands, process, retail, measurement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/industry/consumer-brands*

Czarnowski Collective follows a four-part formula for consumer brand experiences. First, we find the cultural tension and map the full behavioral journey, unpacking the 'why' around the product, the store, and the moment. Second, we design for trial, shareability, and dwell time as one continuous idea, blending tactile design with digital engagement as a single strategic approach. Third, we prototype before scaling, pressure-testing ideas first and then building systems flexible enough to keep their soul as they grow. Fourth, we embed metrics that prove what worked, measuring engagement, conversion, velocity, dwell time, and more. Data is required from the start, not added as an afterthought.

#### Approach to Exhibit Program Management
*Tags: exhibit program, cost management, strategy, process · Source: https://www.czarco.com/careers*

Czarnowski Collective helps clients turn one-off exhibits into a program that pays for itself. The company's Exhibit Program Playbook is designed to help clients determine cost per show, right-size every build, and ensure every asset in a warehouse earns its keep. This approach emphasizes strategic planning and long-term program thinking over individual, disconnected exhibit decisions.

#### Spotting the Booth-by-Booth Trap: Diagnostic Approach
*Tags: diagnostic, exhibit program, trade show, strategy · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-exhibit-program-playbook*

The Exhibit Program Playbook includes a section on spotting the booth-by-booth trap before it compounds. Czarnowski names the warning signs, surfaces the hidden costs, and provides a four-question diagnostic to help brands determine whether they have a true program or simply a calendar of events. The agency notes that the trap does not look like a problem until a brand is several shows deep and the numbers no longer add up.

#### Building a Smarter Exhibit Program: Ownership, Rental, and Hybrid Models
*Tags: exhibit program, ownership, rental, hybrid model · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-exhibit-program-playbook*

Czarnowski Collective's playbook covers the framework for building a strategic exhibit program, including guidance on what is worth owning, what is worth renting, and what is simply taking up warehouse space. The resource introduces a Golden Equation for ownership decisions, a hybrid model framework, and identifies the four things every well-run exhibit program has in common.

#### Busting Exhibit Program Myths: Reframing Event-by-Event Thinking
*Tags: strategy, exhibit program, myths, trade show · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-exhibit-program-playbook*

A dedicated section of the Exhibit Program Playbook addresses the assumptions that keep brands locked into event-by-event decision making. The agency notes that these assumptions are difficult to spot because they all sound like prudence. The playbook walks through each myth and reframes the underlying question brands should actually be asking.

#### How the '50 More Ways We Wonder' Process Works
*Tags: creative process, brainstorming, ideation, methodology · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-50-more-ways-we-wonder*

The '50 More Ways We Wonder' tool follows a four-step process. First, the user brings a real business or creative problem, ideally one that has resisted easy resolution. Second, they open the resource to any page at random, with no required sequence or index. Third, they sit briefly with the prompt even if it seems unrelated to their challenge, as that dissonance is intentional. Fourth, they run their challenge through the new perspective introduced by the prompt and allow fresh thinking to emerge. The process is designed to take approximately ninety seconds of initial discomfort before ideas begin to surface.

#### Seven Considerations for Healthcare Congress Strategy
*Tags: healthcare congress, exhibit planning, strategy, measurement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-healthcare-congress-playbook*

The Healthcare Congress Playbook outlines seven considerations for planning smarter congress experiences. First, start with a person, not a persona: great experiences are designed for real people with limited time and specific motivations, not fictional audience profiles, so planning should center on actual attendee behavior rather than marketing assumptions. Second, route the room with intention: a floor plan should guide curiosity, create meaningful interactions, and support business goals, making layout a strategic tool rather than a logistical exercise. Third, build a system, not a collection: brands, products, and corporate messaging do not have to compete for attention; a connected portfolio experience ensures every touchpoint works together instead of fighting for airtime. Fourth, turn data into decisions: the best measurement plans begin before the event, and defining success early helps teams capture better insights, make smarter decisions, and improve every subsequent congress.

#### Principle 1: Designing with Purpose, Not Just Aesthetics
*Tags: experience design, purposeful design, brand narrative, strategy · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/3-ways-to-design-experiences-that-stick*

Effective experience design goes beyond visual appeal to embed brand values directly into every element of an exhibit. This means making deliberate choices about materials, construction, and layout that reinforce the brand's story and allow visitors to see, feel, and experience a brand's commitments firsthand. The result is a powerful narrative that resonates with audiences on a deeper level.

#### Principle 2: Using Technology to Create More Human Experiences
*Tags: technology, personalization, audience engagement, interactive · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/3-ways-to-design-experiences-that-stick*

Technology should serve human connection rather than overshadow it. By considering factors such as audience values, desires, emotional triggers, decision-making processes, and cultural influences, brands can incorporate technology that creates insightful choice-driven interactions or personalized and shareable content. This approach helps audiences feel seen, making technology a tool for deeper engagement rather than spectacle.

#### Principle 3: Engaging the Senses for Emotional Impact
*Tags: sensory design, immersive experience, emotional engagement, experiential · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/3-ways-to-design-experiences-that-stick*

Immersive, multi-sensory environments place audiences directly in the experience rather than simply showing it to them. By engaging guests across multiple senses, an exhibit can put attendees in their customer's shoes and create memorable, emotionally-driven moments that effectively showcase a brand's innovations and forward-thinking approach.

#### Three Principles for Designing Shareable Brand Experiences
*Tags: experiential design, participation, shareability, brand activation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/after-the-whistle-what-big-game-tv-ads-teach-us-about-experiential-marketing*

Czarnowski applies three principles when designing experiences intended to generate genuine participation and organic sharing. First, people do not share experiences; they share opinions about experiences. That means designing for tension, a surprising idea, or an emotional gut-punch that lingers. If there is no point of view baked in, there is nothing for people to pass along. Second, participation should feel like a reflex, not a requirement. The right question to ask is: what moment would make someone pull out their phone without being prompted? If you have to tell people to participate, you have already lost them. Third, apply the FOMO test: if someone was not there, would they wish they had been? If the answer is yes, participation and shareability have been designed into the DNA of the experience. If not, it is just a moment, and moments without meaning do not travel.

#### Experiential Design Approach: Designing for Belief, Not Just Foot Traffic
*Tags: experiential design, trade shows, interactive, storytelling · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/beyond-the-booth-how-to-create-cultural-impact-at-trade-shows*

Rather than asking what a booth looks like, Czarnowski asks how people participate in a brand story. Movements invite action through several design principles: immersive storytelling that places the visitor inside a brand's mission rather than in front of its product; interactive moments that allow people to contribute, share, or co-create instead of passively consuming; community spaces where attendees connect through the lens of the brand; and sustainable design choices that reinforce values and align with broader cultural conversations. The goal is alignment, not just attention.

#### Key Questions That Drive Czarnowski's Trade Show Strategy
*Tags: strategy, trade shows, brand positioning, process · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/beyond-the-booth-how-to-create-cultural-impact-at-trade-shows*

When developing a trade show presence, Czarnowski frames strategy around three core questions: What do we want people to believe about us? How do we want them to feel when they leave? And what story do we want them to carry with them and share? These questions shift the design brief from physical execution toward cultural and emotional impact, ensuring the resulting experience reflects what the brand stands for, not just what it sells.

#### Exhibit Program Optimization Approach
*Tags: exhibit program, cost efficiency, strategy, trade show · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/inczights-2025-unwrapped-40-of-our-best-brand-experience-thoughts*

Czarnowski Collective offers an Exhibit Program Playbook designed to help clients move beyond one-off exhibit builds and establish a sustainable program. The playbook addresses cost per show, right-sizing each build for the specific engagement, and ensuring every asset stored in a client's warehouse actively earns its keep. The agency's perspective is that most brands fail at least three of four key indicators that separate a true exhibit program from a warehouse full of disconnected, one-off decisions.

#### Intent-Driven Design Process
*Tags: process, strategy, event design, intentional design · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/attention-is-currency*

Czarnowski Collective architects events with the end outcome in mind. The process begins with three guiding questions: What do we want the audience to feel? What do we want them to believe? What do we want them to do afterward? Once those answers are established, every element, from floorplan to storytelling to digital integration, is aligned to support those objectives. Under this approach, a trade show booth becomes a stage for connection, a product launch becomes a narrative moment tying brand, culture, and audience together, and a digital layer becomes an amplifier that extends impact well beyond the event space.

#### Key Ingredients for Effective Animation Storytelling
*Tags: animation, storytelling, creative process, visual design · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/beyond-the-render-the-art-of-storytelling-through-animation*

Effective animation storytelling relies on four core ingredients. First, a clear message: understanding what is being communicated, whether a simple product claim or a broader narrative. Second, a defined hero: this does not have to be a person; a product, idea, or concept can serve as the hero, as long as the audience knows what to focus on. Third, purposeful pacing: rhythm acts as the audience's emotional guide, and small changes in timing can make a significant difference in feeling, even in short or simple animations. Fourth, visual cues that reinforce the message: lighting, camera angles, and color palettes act as subconscious signposts and should be aligned with the intended message.

#### Infusion's Animation Production Process
*Tags: animation, production process, storyboarding, real-time capture · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/beyond-the-render-the-art-of-storytelling-through-animation*

Infusion's animation process starts with story, not software. The team begins by defining what the audience should feel and what action they should take after viewing. Once the message is established, the story is crafted and the appropriate tools are selected based on the story's needs. The team frequently develops storyboards and animatics to bring a script to life through shot composition and the communication of movement before committing to full animation. Simultaneously, key environments and hero models are developed and refined. The process culminates in a final animation with lighting and rendering. For projects that call for it, the team moves directly into real-time performance capture, allowing for immediate feedback and rapid iteration through rough camera placement, props, character positioning, and motion exploration. The approach is always guided by message first, then budget and timeline.

#### Hands-On Product Demos as a Conversion Tactic
*Tags: demos, conversion, trade show, engagement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/connection-to-conversion-the-new-playbook-for-experiential-events*

Allowing attendees to interact directly with a product, whether a tech tool, beauty product, or piece of machinery, builds confidence and removes doubt. Tactile engagement builds trust through transparency, shows product benefits in real time, and opens the door for on-the-spot feedback and questions. Pairing demos with a QR code for instant access to a special offer or signup form streamlines the path to conversion.

#### Best Practices for Designing a High-Impact Experiential Zone
*Tags: design, process, experiential, best practices · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/experiential-zones-more-brand-experience-bang-for-your-buck*

Designing a small space that delivers big impact follows several key principles. First, tell one great story: resist the urge to communicate everything and instead focus on a single narrative or value proposition that can be explored in depth. Second, involve the senses, as the most memorable experiences activate sight, sound, and touch, and sometimes even taste or smell. Third, design for dwell by incorporating seating, shade, and small touches that invite people to linger. Fourth, think social by considering lighting, sightlines, and brand moments designed for content creation and sharing. Fifth, build for the future using modular builds, lightweight materials, and smart shipping strategies that make it easy to reuse and repurpose assets.

#### Scene Optimization for Energy-Efficient Rendering
*Tags: rendering, sustainability, optimization, 3d production · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/green-rendering-how-3d-studios-can-reduce-their-environmental-footprint*

A core sustainable rendering practice is cleaning up the scene before hitting render. Hidden objects, unused materials, and overly detailed geometry slow output and waste energy. Organizing files and optimizing geometry results in faster render times and reduced energy consumption. Not every background asset requires 4K textures or hyper-detail; computing power should be focused on elements that drive the story. Selective detail allocation is both a creative and environmental decision.

#### Draft and Preview Rendering to Reduce Energy Use
*Tags: rendering, workflow, sustainability, draft review · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/green-rendering-how-3d-studios-can-reduce-their-environmental-footprint*

Infusion's sustainable rendering workflow emphasizes using the right level of fidelity for each stage of production. For in-progress animations, quick viewport captures help refine flow without the cost of full rendering. Draft renders for review can use lower resolution or a higher noise threshold and still serve effectively for client feedback. White model renders, which skip textures and materials entirely, are recommended when the goal is layout or composition review. These approaches are faster, more sustainable, and keep creative conversations focused. Full renders are reserved for when they truly matter.

#### Off-Peak and Farm Management for Greener Online Rendering
*Tags: render farm, sustainability, energy efficiency, production management · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/green-rendering-how-3d-studios-can-reduce-their-environmental-footprint*

Energy-intensive rendering workloads can be managed more sustainably through scheduling and infrastructure discipline. Rendering during off-peak hours takes advantage of times when energy grids are both greener and less expensive. Render farm management software such as Deadline can power down idle machines and wake them only when needed, reducing unnecessary energy draw. The same tools enable teams to monitor CPU usage and identify inefficiencies before they compound into larger problems.

#### Streamlined Review Process to Reduce Rework and Energy Waste
*Tags: review process, client collaboration, efficiency, rendering · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/green-rendering-how-3d-studios-can-reduce-their-environmental-footprint*

A disorganized feedback loop burns both time and energy. Infusion's approach to sustainable rendering includes a defined review process that aligns client expectations upfront around review tools and timing. Centralized platforms such as Ftrack, Wrike, and Frame.io reduce confusion and repeat work by consolidating feedback in one place. A consistent, well-defined process enables the team to deliver smarter rather than harder, cutting unnecessary render cycles driven by miscommunication.

#### Four Pillars for Navigating the Shift to Experience-Led Environments
*Tags: process, experiential design, physical digital integration, measurement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/how-the-experience-economy-is-taking-over-the-showroom*

Czarnowski recommends four guiding pillars for brands making the shift from traditional showrooms to experience-led engagement. First, think beyond your four walls: mobile, pop-up, and hybrid formats are all part of the new landscape. Second, design for experience first, crafting how people walk in, engage, move, and share rather than leading with the product alone. Third, bridge physical and digital by integrating physical environments such as structures, fabrication, and spatial design with digital experiences including immersive tech, AR, and mixed reality to create seamless journeys. Fourth, measure and iterate using engagement rates, dwell time, and content shares to refine the approach over time, with the goal of sustained relevance rather than a single one-off activation.

#### Four-Point Playbook for Turning Fans into Brand Advocates
*Tags: sports marketing, experiential, process, fan engagement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/international-soccer-and-the-future-of-experiential-sports-marketing*

Converting fans into long-term brand advocates requires a deliberate experiential strategy built on four principles. First, create presence, not just placement, meaning the brand should be experienced, not just seen. Second, design for emotion, ensuring every light, sound, and interaction makes fans feel something real. Third, integrate seamlessly so that when technology and design work together the experience feels natural rather than forced. Fourth, keep the story going by extending the best activations beyond the venue and continuing the connection both online and in real life.

#### Three Design Principles for High-Impact Microengagements
*Tags: design principles, experiential, engagement, emotion · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/microengagements-why-the-future-of-experiences-is-measured-in-seconds*

Designing meaningful experiences in a short window comes down to three core elements. First, clarity over cleverness: the hook should land within seconds, communicating what is being offered and why the audience should care, through visual storytelling, kinetic content, and visceral cues. Second, emotion first: microengagements have no time for a slow build. Leading with feeling, whether joy, nostalgia, excitement, or surprise, is the key to resonance. Third, tactile and digital balance: blending physical and digital elements anchors memory and boosts interaction. This can include QR codes used with purpose, light-triggered motion, or audio textures that draw people in quickly.

#### What separates a real exhibit program from ad-hoc decisions
*Tags: exhibit program, strategy, assessment, trade show · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/making-bold-ideas-happen-irl*

The agency identifies four key indicators that separate a genuine, managed exhibit program from a warehouse full of one-off decisions. Brands that lack a true program typically fail at least three of these four criteria. Evaluating against these criteria is a practical starting point for diagnosing and improving exhibit program maturity.

#### Design Process Principle: Prototype Experiences Like Game Levels
*Tags: process, prototyping, experience design, iteration · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/gdc-2026-what-experiential-marketing-can-learn-from-gaming*

Czarnowski advocates for a game-development-inspired prototyping process for live experiences. Practical steps include writing a one-page player manual that defines who the attendee is, what they want, and what success looks like for them; running a low-fidelity prototype of the entry sequence with a small group and adjusting based on where people stall or disengage; and selecting one micro-detail, such as a scent moment, a line of copy, or a hidden interaction, to over-invest in with the obsession a game developer would bring. This iterative, player-centric process helps teams identify friction points before an experience goes live.

#### How to Evaluate a Rental Exhibit Partner
*Tags: vendor selection, rentals, exhibit strategy, trade shows · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/rentals-are-for-brands-that-cant-afford-custom-and-other-things-that-arent-true*

A meaningful rental exhibit partner opens the conversation with strategy before price. The right questions to ask up front are about a client's show calendar, warehouse situation, and goals for the year. A partner who starts there is selling a program. A partner who opens with a discount or frames the conversation primarily around cost savings is treating the booth as a commodity purchase. When a vendor leads with price, they have signaled exactly how they think about the client's exhibit presence.

#### Designing Experiences with Purpose: Five Guiding Principles
*Tags: process, experiential design, strategy, engagement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/more-than-just-a-pretty-space-designing-experiences-with-purpose*

Czarnowski approaches purposeful experience design through five principles. First, start with the 'why': before addressing aesthetics, define the purpose of the space, whether launching a product, reinforcing brand values, or fostering community, and let that intent inform every design decision. Second, make it interactive: people want to participate, not just observe, so interactive design invites audiences to engage and creates deeper connections. Third, design for flow: intentional wayfinding and spatial design guide visitors effortlessly, avoiding confusion or chaos. Fourth, appeal to emotion: the most memorable experiences evoke emotions through storytelling, ambiance, or surprise elements. Fifth, measure the impact: define KPIs from the start, such as foot traffic, dwell time, social shares, or sales conversions, so purposeful design delivers measurable results.

#### Collaborative Process Behind Temporary Structures
*Tags: collaboration, design process, fabrication, temporary structures · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/temporary-architecture-is-the-future-of-brand-storytelling-heres-why*

Behind every successful temporary structure is seamless collaboration. Architects, designers, engineers, and builders align from concept to execution, translating bold ideas into real, movable environments. The process demands precision, problem-solving, and a shared understanding that the goal is not simply to build something that stands, but to build something that works everywhere it goes.

#### Design for a Build, Not Just a Bang
*Tags: experiential design, narrative, event strategy, engagement · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-bold-case-for-waiting-3-ways-experiential-marketers-can-use-anticipation-as-a-strategy*

Rather than engineering one oversized 'wow' moment, effective experiential design thinks in acts. The approach involves a progression: a first breadcrumb that draws audiences in, layers that deepen the story, and a final reveal that only rewards those who stay. Without progression, there is no payoff, and without payoff, there is nothing for the audience to remember. Structuring an experience this way creates tension and a reason for attendees to remain engaged.

#### Making Access Feel Earned Through Participation
*Tags: experiential, audience engagement, event design, participation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-bold-case-for-waiting-3-ways-experiential-marketers-can-use-anticipation-as-a-strategy*

Access within an experience should feel earned through participation, not blocked through gatekeeping. This can take several forms: a final reveal that unlocks only after meaningful interaction, a VIP moment that occurs at a specific hour, or a best-in-show element that is not visible from the aisle. When audiences invest time in an experience, they invest emotionally. That emotional investment is what converts 'cool' into 'I can't stop thinking about it.'

#### Leaving Space for Curiosity in Experiential Design
*Tags: experiential design, creative strategy, discovery, messaging · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-bold-case-for-waiting-3-ways-experiential-marketers-can-use-anticipation-as-a-strategy*

Over-explaining kills intrigue. When every message, feature, and benefit is spelled out the moment an attendee walks in, there is no opportunity for discovery, and discovery is what makes an experience stick. The guiding question for any activation should be: are we leading people through something, or dumping information on them immediately? Curiosity is the hook, and effective experiential design lets it work rather than short-circuiting it with over-communication.

#### Framework: How Brands Can Break the Cycle of Safe Creative
*Tags: creative process, brand strategy, experiential, collaboration · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-death-of-the-ordinary-why-safe-kills-brand-presence*

Breaking the habit of safe creative starts with a mindset shift and five practical principles. First, start with honesty: identify what the brand truly stands for, not what is trending or convenient, and ensure every touchpoint reflects that truth. Second, embrace imperfection, because audiences crave authenticity, not polish; showing process, people, and passion resonates more than a flawless facade. Third, design for emotion rather than approval; if a campaign does not make someone feel something, it has not gone far enough. Fourth, create space for risk by building internal processes that reward experimentation instead of punishing it, giving bold ideas room to breathe. Fifth, collaborate without compromise: the best work comes when strategy and creativity challenge each other, not when they meet in the middle. When brands commit to this mindset, creative teams become more engaged, campaigns hit harder, and audiences respond with energy instead of indifference.

#### Five Tactical Principles for Designing in Distracted Environments
*Tags: creative strategy, experience design, engagement, activation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/we-spent-two-hours-at-ems-proving-our-own-point*

Czarnowski's Executive Creative Director, Joneric Amundson, developed a framework of five tactical principles for breaking through in crowded, distracted environments: pattern interrupts that make sense, action before explanation, visible payoff, low commitment with high curiosity, and curiosity gaps. The underlying philosophy is to reduce friction, increase intrigue, and reward quickly. The most effective pattern interrupts use familiar structures with unexpected behavior rather than inventing entirely new formats. A practical test: if someone cannot understand what to do from 15 feet away in under two seconds, the design has already failed.

#### Early Strategic Engagement: What Real Partnership Looks Like
*Tags: strategy, exhibit, attendee experience, planning · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/your-vendor-is-already-panicking-about-ces-2027-your-partner-isnt*

A true experiential partner engages before the brief exists. They ask the questions an internal team may not have reached yet: What is the narrative arc of the space? Where does the attendee journey begin, before they even step on the floor? How does the exhibit work at 9am with press versus 3pm with buyers? What contingency plans exist when technology fails on-site? By establishing this strategic framework early, every downstream creative and production decision becomes easier and smarter, and the brand story lands with the people who matter most.

#### Portfolio Approach to Exhibit Program Management
*Tags: exhibit program, portfolio management, rentals, trade show strategy · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/your-exhibit-is-a-depreciating-asset-your-program-doesnt-have-to-be*

High-performing brands manage their exhibit spend like a portfolio rather than a collection of one-off decisions. The model includes a small core of owned assets that genuinely earn ownership through high utilization, stable footprint, and brand elements with a long shelf life. Surrounding that core is a flexible layer of rentals sized to each show and each year. The portfolio is rebalanced annually, with utilization data rather than sentiment determining what stays owned, what converts to rental, and what goes to consignment.

#### Four Habits of a Financially Efficient Exhibit Program
*Tags: exhibit program, process, utilization, cost management · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/your-exhibit-is-a-depreciating-asset-your-program-doesnt-have-to-be*

A well-run exhibit program is characterized by four consistent practices. First, per-show costs are known in advance rather than reconstructed after the fact. Second, utilization is tracked per asset so that every item in the warehouse can justify its storage rent. Third, the owned-versus-rental mix is rebalanced once a year. Fourth, a single partner can see and quote the entire show calendar. These habits allow organizations to keep exhibit spending transparent, accountable, and optimized across the full program.

#### Exhibit Program Assessment: Four Diagnostic Questions
*Tags: exhibit program, assessment, process, strategy · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/sxsw-2026-we-took-notes-so-your-strategy-didnt-have-to*

Czarnowski Collective has identified four key indicators that separate a genuine, well-managed exhibit program from a warehouse of disconnected, one-off decisions. The agency offers a four-question exhibit program test to help brands self-diagnose, noting that most brands fail at least three of the four criteria. This diagnostic framing underpins the agency's broader exhibit program strategy work.

### credential (66)

#### Selected Client Work and Scale
*Tags: credentials, case studies, client work, events · Source: https://www.czarco.com*

Czarnowski Collective's portfolio includes large-scale, high-profile brand experiences. Themes from the festival included brands pushing creative boundaries, technology taking center stage, immersive activations, and unexpected brand collaborations.

*Tags: rentals, client data, exhibit program, large exhibitors · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/your-exhibit-is-a-depreciating-asset-your-program-doesnt-have-to-be*

### story (112)

#### Formation of Czarnowski Collective
*Tags: czarnowski collective, agency evolution, experiential, brand experiences · Source: https://www.czarco.com/about*

In 2019, Czarnowski restructured its operating model by forming Czarnowski Collective, an integrated group of specialized studios created to deliver immersive brand experiences. The move was a direct response to the evolving landscape of experiential marketing and eliminated the legacy agency model in favor of a structure with four studios, one banner, and no internal silos. This evolution expanded the company's offerings to include temporary structures, mobile activations, influencer programs, and hybrid experiences, cementing its position as a leader in the experiential space.

#### Historical Milestones and Industry Firsts
*Tags: history, milestones, growth, trade shows · Source: https://www.czarco.com/about*

Czarnowski was founded in Chicago in 1947 and built an early reputation for reliability and craftsmanship in trade show exhibit construction. During the post-war manufacturing boom of the 1950s and 1960s, the company expanded its focus to architecture and engineering, becoming a trusted fabricator for growing American industries. In the 1970s and 1980s, the company opened additional U.S. offices and added integrated logistics and labor support. By the 1990s, Czarnowski expanded into creative, design, program strategy, and consulting. In the 2000s, the company moved engineering and fabrication in-house and launched global trade show programs. By the 2010s, Czarnowski had become a trusted partner to Fortune 500 brands for large-scale activations at events such as CES and SXSW, completing its transformation into a full-fledged brand experience agency.

At NYCxDESIGN 2025, Infusion created an immersive brand experience for Lexus that went beyond passive display. The experience invited the design community directly into the Lexus brand world.

As a newly-minted NFL sponsor, Toyota needed its debut Super Bowl activation to cut through a crowded field of competitors and make a memorable impact at scale. Czarnowski Collective designed a thrilling fan experience built around four truck-themed territories where participants had 58 seconds to find a winning game ball hidden among 58,000 footballs, complemented by a photo moment via the NFL OnePass app and full creative and event production for Toyota's Radio Row presence. The activation drew 5,084 participants in the four-down territory challenge and produced 189 game winners, delivering a brand moment that felt genuinely larger than life.

Two large-scale fan activations delivered in 24 days for WrestleMania 42

With only 24 days until WrestleMania 42, Saudi Arabia's General Entertainment Authority needed two fan activations built and ready to preview WrestleMania's 2026 move to Saudi Arabia. Czarnowski, working alongside HQ Worldwide Shows, functioned as a single integrated team to compress months of coordination into weeks, delivering one experience inside the Las Vegas Convention Center and a second high-traffic activation outside Allegiant Stadium. Thousands of fans engaged with both activations across WrestleMania Week, with both builds completed on time and without compromising quality or craftsmanship.

#### Yves Saint Laurent - Yves Saint Laurent Pop-Up Retail Experience
*Tags: case-study, pop-up retail, luxury, fabrication, freight container, Yves Saint Laurent · Source: https://www.czarco.com/work/yves-saint-laurent-pop-up-retail-experience*

Luxury in a box: two freight containers transformed into a YSL flagship-worthy retail space

Yves Saint Laurent needed a high-end retail presence on Germany's prestigious Koenigsallee without a permanent storefront. In partnership with Severich and Partner, Czarnowski Collective transformed two standard freight containers into a luxury pop-up store, complete with marble-style finishes, oversized gilded florals, and a warm lighting scheme reflective of the iconic YSL brand. The result was a space that seamlessly integrated into one of Europe's most exclusive shopping streets, demonstrating that a temporary structure can deliver a fully premium retail experience.

#### Toyota - Toyota National Dealer Meeting 2025
*Tags: case-study, automotive, dealer meeting, experiential, event design, regional strategy · Source: https://www.czarco.com/work/toyota-national-dealer-meeting-2025*

95% attendance and 2+ hour average dwell time at Toyota's immersive regional dealer celebrations

Toyota needed a dealer meeting experience that celebrated the individuality of each Region while reinforcing a unified brand identity across its entire dealer network. Czarnowski Collective developed an insights-driven program with mapped guest journeys, regional production design, culinary storytelling, custom activations, and live entertainment that gave every Region its own spotlight while keeping the narrative cohesive. #### Creative Brainstorming Resource: 50 Ways We Wonder
*Tags: creative process, brainstorming, ideation, resources · Source: https://www.czarco.com/resource/50-ways-we-wonder*

Czarnowski Collective has developed a creative field guide called '50 More Ways We Wonder,' described as a resource for creative dead ends. The guide offers 50 unexpected prompts designed to reframe problems and move teams toward fresher, more original answers. It reflects the agency's broader commitment to structured creative thinking and problem reframing as part of their process.

#### Bringing Complexity to Life: RefleXion Case Example
*Tags: health and life science, exhibit, case example, storytelling · Source: https://www.czarco.com/lp/download-healthcare-congress-playbook*

For RefleXion's biology-guided radiotherapy machine, which weighed six tons, the team brought the idea of the machine to the congress floor. The result demonstrated that representing a concept compellingly can make a stronger impact than displaying the physical product itself.

#### Case Study: Panasonic Green Impact at CES 2024
*Tags: sustainability, CES, Panasonic, exhibit design, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/3-ways-to-design-experiences-that-stick*

At CES 2024, the agency helped Panasonic promote its Green Impact initiative by designing an experience that went beyond messaging and interactive stations. The team re-purposed existing Panasonic booth elements, utilized sustainable supplementary materials such as Repreve fabrics and PVC-free vinyl, standardized sizing to promote and protect future reusability, and enacted efficiency practices that minimized the carbon footprint during booth setup. This approach allowed visitors to see, feel, and experience the brand's commitment to eco-friendly practices, creating a powerful narrative that resonated with audiences on a deeper level.

#### Case Study: Coca-Cola Immersive Retail Environment at NACS
*Tags: Coca-Cola, retail, NACS, immersive, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/3-ways-to-design-experiences-that-stick*

At NACS, the agency helped Coca-Cola create an immersive mini-mart-style environment that gave retailers firsthand experience with the consumer trends and shifting habits of their shoppers. By engaging the senses of guests and placing them in their customer's shoes, the exhibit created a memorable, emotionally-driven experience that effectively showcased use cases for Coca-Cola's latest innovations and the brand's forward-thinking approach to helping convenience stores thrive in a rapidly changing market.

#### Case Study: Lexus Ambiance at NYCxDesign
*Tags: lexus, automotive, interactive, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarnowski-collective-named-one-of-chief-marketers-agencies-of-the-year*

For Lexus at NYCxDesign in Hudson Yards, New York, Czarnowski Collective created the Ambiance activation anchored by the GX 550 and an LC 500 Convertible wrapped in original artwork. The experience blurred the line between vehicle display and digital installation. Motion-sensing screens transformed visitors' movements into vivid light-based art that could be downloaded and shared. Those movements also affected the entire installation in real time, shifting sound, color, and texture of the space. Ambiance marked a new kind of brand moment for Lexus: not just premium, but participatory, delivering a visceral expression of what it means to Experience Amazing.

#### Case Study: Museum of the Future at SXSW 2025
*Tags: sxsw, technology, cultural experience, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarnowski-collective-named-one-of-chief-marketers-agencies-of-the-year*

When the Dubai Future Foundation's Museum of the Future debuted at South by Southwest (SXSW) for the first time, Czarnowski Collective built an activation as bold as its mission. The team constructed a 60-foot by 100-foot structure in Austin that served as a beacon for attendees. Inside, a 360-degree LED environment from artist Refik Anadol, fueled by AI-driven data, wrapped guests in living, breathing dreamscapes. Outside, a backyard-style hangout offered Dubai Chocolate, Middle Eastern calligraphy, and live Chai tea demonstrations. A 120-seat thought leadership space hosted voices from Arab cinema to the first Emirati astronaut, exploring the intersection of culture, technology, and the future. Strategy, storytelling, and precision came together to build a moment that stayed with people long after SXSW.

#### Case Study: Coca-Cola Neighborhood at NACS 2024
*Tags: coca-cola, consumer brands, sustainability, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarnowski-collective-named-one-of-chief-marketers-agencies-of-the-year*

At NACS 2024, Czarnowski Collective transformed a 5,000-square-foot footprint into a vibrant Coca-Cola Neighborhood. Each brand in the Coca-Cola lineup claimed its own corner, pairing immersive design with hands-on moments to guide guests through a lively, memorable journey. The space showcased new innovations including Coca-Cola Spiced, Orange Cream Coke, and Sprite Plus Tea, while creating smart touchpoints for deeper consumer connection. Czarnowski Collective's design and fabrication teams also elevated Coca-Cola's sustainability narrative by integrating reusable sampling cups developed with r.Cup and advanced recycling stations built with Oscar Sort seamlessly into the Neighborhood, demonstrating that responsibility and creativity can share the same address.

#### End-to-End Experiential Event Execution: A Real-World Example
*Tags: experiential, event planning, brand activation, execution · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/connection-to-conversion-the-new-playbook-for-experiential-events*

A well-rounded experiential activation integrates multiple engagement layers simultaneously. For a fitness product launch themed 'The Future of Fitness,' this could mean setting up a mini workout station for product testing, hosting short high-energy demo sessions every hour, offering a branded smoothie bar adjacent to the activation, encouraging visitors to photograph themselves at a custom branded backdrop, and positioning team members nearby with tablets to answer questions and offer promo codes. Attendees leave not with a brochure, but with a story, a taste, a feeling, and a strong reason to reconnect.

#### Kubota CES 2026 Exhibit: Agricultural Innovation Experience
*Tags: kubota, ces, agricultural technology, immersive exhibit · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarco-ces-2026-how-we-took-over-the-show-floor*

For Kubota at CES 2026, Czarnowski Collective designed an environment that balanced advanced technology with natural familiarity, transporting visitors from familiar fields into the future of farming without losing sight of the human at the center of it all. Exhibit highlights included a commercial autonomous tractor, a concept transformer robot, and an interactive Digital Twin table that allowed attendees to explore smart agricultural solutions in real time. Sensory details reinforced the narrative: subtle scents of fresh grass and soil, cinematic footage captured in Napa and Japan, and interfaces designed to feel accessible rather than overwhelming. From app interactions to flexible machine control concepts, the experience reinforced Kubota's belief that technology should extend a farmer's expertise, not replace it. The result was an exhibit described as grounded, immersive, and genuinely human.

#### LG CES 2026 Exhibit: Large-Scale Production and Execution
*Tags: LG, ces, fabrication, large-scale exhibit · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarco-ces-2026-how-we-took-over-the-show-floor*

Czarnowski served as LG's trusted production and execution partner at CES 2026, translating complex creative vision into high-impact physical form. From early engineering conversations through fabrication and on-site delivery, the Czarnowski team value-engineered every square inch of the booth. The goal was not simply efficiency but elevation, with quality, safety, and precision embedded at every step to ensure LG's design standards were not only met but enhanced. By aligning early and collaborating closely, Czarnowski helped LG deliver a CES presence that felt seamless, intentional, and unmistakably premium. The engagement demonstrated that large-scale exhibits demand more than craftsmanship: they require coordination, adaptability, and a deep understanding of how design decisions play out in the real world.

#### Panasonic CES 2026 Exhibit: AI Storytelling Through Immersive Zones
*Tags: panasonic, ces, AI, immersive zones · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/czarco-ces-2026-how-we-took-over-the-show-floor*

For Panasonic at CES 2026, Czarnowski Collective partnered from strategy through show floor execution to bring the theme 'The Future We Make' to life. The exhibit guided visitors through three immersive zones: 'The Future We Optimize,' which explored how AI can streamline operations and sharpen decision-making; 'The Future We Safeguard,' which highlighted sustainability, resource protection, and waste reduction; and 'The Future We Live,' which showcased AI applications in wellness, fitness, and life-enhancing technology. An immersive entry experience set the tone by drawing visitors into the story, while a playful, participatory exit ensured the message stayed with them after leaving the booth. The resulting exhibit was designed to feel optimistic, engaging, and deeply relevant.

#### Czarnowski Collective Sports Industry POV
*Tags: sports, industry event, thought leadership, czarnowski · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/fan-experience-is-no-longer-a-perk-its-the-product*

The Czarnowski Collective team attended CAA SBJ's World Congress of Sports in Los Angeles, where the conversation among private equity partners, media executives, architects, and franchise leaders was execution-focused and deal-oriented rather than theoretical. Across panels and hallway conversations, five consistent themes emerged: fan experience as a core asset, teams operating as media companies, smarter and more demanding investment, personalization as a baseline expectation, and an industry-wide sense of urgency to move from discussion to action.

#### Hands-On Experiential Activation: The assembly Line Case Study
*Tags: trade show, activation, assembly line, experiential · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/hands-on-is-the-future-of-experiential-we-built-a-booth-to-prove-it*

At EMS, the assembly brand launched an activation called The assembly Line to demonstrate its temporary structures capabilities in a direct, tactile way. Rather than explaining what assembly does through signage or collateral, the team put a miniature version of the product directly into attendees' hands. Guests checked in on an iPad, then followed The assembly Line to build their own miniature Delta Vista structure. The kit was designed in partnership with Bricker Builds of Marietta, GA, and featured horizontal Vista windows, a removable top and front panel to set an interior scene, and a custom-printed assembly logo tile on the roof. An accessories bin offered plants, chairs, lamps, and coffee mugs, and attendees finished by placing a minifig inside to put themselves in the scene. The result was hundreds of kits handed out, with attendees sitting down and building on-site, unprompted, on a trade show floor.

#### Expo West Trade Show Trends: Attention and Engagement on the Show Floor
*Tags: expo west, trade show, brand activation, consumer brands · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/expo-west-the-battle-for-attention-and-bag-space*

At Natural Products Expo West, attention is the primary currency. With thousands of brands competing for the same buyers, retailers, and media, success comes down to who can stop an attendee in the aisle and keep them there long enough to create a memory. Key themes observed across the show floor include the power of branded giveaways, the limits of photo moments in small footprints, sampling as the central activation, home-inspired visual motifs, theatrical disruption, experiential design visibility, and sensory atmosphere. The brands that broke through shared a willingness to commit fully to a single, clear idea rather than trying to do too much in their footprint.

#### Theatrical Disruption as a Brand Strategy at Trade Shows
*Tags: experiential, brand disruption, trade show, consumer brands · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/expo-west-the-battle-for-attention-and-bag-space*

Disruptor brands that lean fully into theatrical and bold activations consistently generate attention at crowded trade shows. At Expo West, O Positiv built a booth designed to make a statement about vaginal health awareness, featuring a giant inflatable entrance, a shaped mirror photo moment, oversized hanging product containers, and bold unapologetic messaging. Whether attendees loved it or felt uncomfortable, the booth accomplished exactly what disruptor brands aim for: it made people stop. Brands willing to push boundaries and invite cultural conversation create the most memorable moments on the show floor.

#### Why Traditional Showrooms Are Losing Relevance
*Tags: automotive, tech, consumer behavior, showroom · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/how-the-experience-economy-is-taking-over-the-showroom*

Three structural pressures are eroding the traditional showroom model. First, buyers today often start their journey online, configuring, comparing, and deciding before stepping into a physical space. Second, younger audiences do not want to be sold to in sterile environments; they want to explore, experiment, and feel connected. Third, showrooms are expensive, fixed, and static, and they do not always map to where influence and attention actually live. These pressures apply equally to automotive and tech brands, and they signal that the four-wall showroom alone is no longer sufficient.

#### Perspective on Exhibit Assets as Depreciating vs. Programmatic Investments
*Tags: exhibit program, asset management, strategy, ROI · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/inczights-2025-unwrapped-40-of-our-best-brand-experience-thoughts*

Czarnowski Collective takes the position that a single exhibit is a depreciating asset, but a well-structured exhibit program does not have to be. By approaching exhibits as part of an ongoing program rather than isolated builds, brands can manage costs more effectively and extend the strategic value of each asset over time.

#### Perspective on Rental Exhibits
*Tags: rentals, exhibit strategy, custom vs. rental, brand experience · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/inczights-2025-unwrapped-40-of-our-best-brand-experience-thoughts*

Czarnowski Collective challenges the common misconception that rental exhibits are only for brands that cannot afford custom builds. The agency advocates for rentals as a legitimate, strategic choice that can serve brands across budget levels and program structures.

#### Adele Munich Residency: Temporary Arena Case Study
*Tags: temporary structures, case study, sustainability, live events · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/next-big-thing-in-pop-culture-temporary-structures*

For her 2024 residency in Munich, Germany, Adele installed a custom-built temporary arena featuring a Guinness World Record-breaking 721 by 98-foot LED screen to support a 10-show run. The venue was erected in just 30 days, cost less than $135 million to build, and generated nearly $600 million in profit. The residency became the highest-attended concert residency outside of Las Vegas, with more than 730,000 tickets sold. Many of the materials used to create the arena were designed to be recycled and reused after the event, and ticket packages included public transit access to help reduce the transportation footprint associated with an audience of that scale.

#### Paris 2024 Olympics: Sustainability Through Temporary Construction
*Tags: temporary structures, sustainability, olympics, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/next-big-thing-in-pop-culture-temporary-structures*

The 2024 Paris Olympics were organized around the theme of doing more with less, with organizers striving to deliver an environmentally responsible experience across venues, energy usage, transportation, and food. The games aimed to cut their carbon footprint by 50% compared to the London 2012 and Rio 2016 games, a goal aligned with the Paris Agreement. An astonishing 95% of the venues used were created using temporary construction. Spectator seats were made from recycled plastic, and solar panels served as the primary power source for the structures, illustrating how large-scale events can achieve both memorability and sustainability.

#### Events Industry Waste Context and assembly's Response
*Tags: events industry, waste reduction, sustainability, client trust · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/sustainability-isnt-a-choice-its-our-foundation*

The events industry generates approximately 2.5 pounds of waste per person per day, a figure that raises urgent questions for marketers about how to protect the planet without compromising creativity. assembly's response is not a reactive policy shift but a reflection of how the company has always operated: sustainability is structural, not situational. This positions assembly's clients to demonstrate meaningful environmental action to their own audiences, building customer trust and loyalty in the process.

#### BroadRoom Denver Launch Event Details
*Tags: denver, launch, community, women in events · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-broadroom-x-czarco-launches-in-denver*

The inaugural BroadRoom event launched in Denver. The evening began at Czarnowski Collective's Denver facility, where eight women gathered for introductions and conversation. The group then moved to Holidaily Brewing Company in Golden, a woman-owned business, before attending a live comedy show at Red Rocks Amphitheatre. The event brought together Czarnowski Collective team members alongside an executive from a tech company, a local mental health advocate, and an event industry partner.

#### POV on Exhibit Programs vs. One-Off Decisions
*Tags: exhibit strategy, POV, trade shows, program management · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

Czarnowski Collective has published a perspective arguing that most brands fail at distinguishing a real exhibit program from a warehouse full of one-off decisions. Their content identifies four specific indicators that separate a structured exhibit program from ad hoc exhibit spending, positioning the agency as a strategic partner rather than simply a builder.

#### POV on Rental Exhibits
*Tags: rentals, exhibit strategy, POV, trade shows · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

Czarnowski Collective challenges the common misconception that rental exhibits are only for brands that cannot afford custom builds. Their published perspective argues that this assumption is not true, positioning rentals as a legitimate and strategic option for brands of all sizes.

#### Exhibit Assets as Depreciating Investments
*Tags: exhibit program, asset management, strategy, trade shows · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

Czarnowski Collective has published a perspective noting that while an individual exhibit is a depreciating asset, a well-structured exhibit program does not have to be. This framing positions program thinking as the way brands protect and extend the value of their exhibit investments over time.

#### Sports Marketing POV: Girls Flag Football
*Tags: sports marketing, fan engagement, emerging trends, POV · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

Czarnowski Collective has identified girls' flag football as a significant untapped opportunity in sports marketing. According to their published analysis, girls' flag football is growing at 60% year-over-year, yet most brands have not yet engaged with the space. The agency positions this as one of the biggest emerging opportunities for brands active in sports sponsorship and fan engagement.

#### AI and Data in Brand Environments
*Tags: AI, technology, brand environments, thought leadership · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

*Tags: temporary structures, modular design, trends, POV · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights*

Czarnowski Collective has published a point of view arguing that temporary structures are becoming the next big thing in pop culture. The agency offers temporary structures as a distinct service line and has developed editorial content framing this capability as a forward-looking opportunity for brands.

#### Case Study: adidas 'Home of Soccer' Experiential Activation
*Tags: experiential, sports, fan engagement, activation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/why-brands-are-all-running-the-same-play-for-soccers-biggest-tournament*

adidas built the 'Home of Soccer,' a 25,000-square-foot free public space at Brooklyn Bridge Park open daily from June 13 through July 19, featuring match screenings, a small-sided pitch, and live concerts. Similar setups ran in Toronto, LA, Houston, Atlanta, and across Mexico. The activation extended the brand's campaign thesis that the best soccer happens outside the stadium, handing the space over to whoever showed up for the entire length of the tournament. The surrounding product program reinforced the same idea of meeting the fan where their version of soccer culture actually lives, ranging from a luxury lifestyle collection to jerseys hand-embroidered by more than 150 indigenous female artisans.

#### Case Study: Nike Physical Fan Spaces Across Host Cities
*Tags: experiential, sports, fan engagement, multi-city activation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/why-brands-are-all-running-the-same-play-for-soccers-biggest-tournament*

Nike built a physical layer of fan spaces across every major host city for the 2026 tournament, including the House of Merc in New York, Estadio Niky's in LA, and The Roof at Vanta in Dallas. These spaces were designed around watching, playing, and buying. The strategy was supported by the Toma el Juego grassroots street soccer platform, which ran more than 100 tournaments across six continents and livestreamed its 2026 finals on Amazon. Nike's approach demonstrates that a path to category leadership runs through intentional physical spaces, not just splashy digital media.

#### Case Study: New Balance Pop-Up Strategy Without Official Sponsorship
*Tags: experiential, sports, pop-up, brand activation · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/why-brands-are-all-running-the-same-play-for-soccers-biggest-tournament*

New Balance does not sponsor a single national team in the 2026 tournament, yet built a meaningful experiential presence by running a rolling pop-up across Boston, LA, Miami, and New York. The spaces were anchored by live Men in Blazers podcast recordings and a boot atelier, described as intimate rooms designed for fans who already know why they are there. The brand activated ten athletes competing in the tournament, including Bukayo Saka and Endrick, through a timed Stone Island collaboration capsule. The case illustrates that an official tournament badge is not required to build a credible physical presence; rooms full of people who care turn out to be enough.

### reference (1)

#### Case Study Reference: Purina and St. Louis City SC Brand Authenticity
*Tags: sports, sponsorship, brand authenticity, case study · Source: https://www.czarco.com/insights/the-sports-industry-has-an-innovation-theater-problem-and-we-have-receipts*

Purina built a local partnership with St. Louis City SC anchored entirely around what is authentic to their brand: pets. That focused, authentic approach grew into a national relationship with U.S. Soccer. The lesson is that radical authenticity at scale, without gimmicks and without scope creep, can be a powerful and replicable growth engine for sports sponsors.

### Akamai - Akamai Edge World Conference and Expo
*ARIA Hotel, Las Vegas · Full-service conference and expo design and execution, from creative strategy through on-site delivery*

**So good they made it annual: 6,000 sharp minds, zero standard conference energy**

**Challenge.** Akamai needed their four-day annual gathering of 6,000 developers, technologists, and industry influencers to feel nothing like a standard conference, appealing to an audience that craves connection, competition, and memorable experiences over passive learning

**Resolution.** Czarnowski Collective transformed the ARIA hotel into an immersive environment with a circular non-linear expo floor, color-coded lighting, dedicated demo stations, a theater, and unexpected engagement features including an escape room, golf simulator, and arcade, all integrated as content extensions rather than afterthoughts

**Outcome.** The event was so successful it became an annual event for Akamai

Akamai needed Edge World, their four-day conference for 6,000 developers and technologists, to feel like anything but a conference. Czarnowski Collective transformed the ARIA hotel in Las Vegas into a fabled portal to the future, building a circular non-linear expo floor with zone-specific lighting and graphics, plus an escape room, golf simulator, and arcade as genuine content features. The result was compelling enough that Akamai brought Czarnowski Collective back to make Edge World an annual event.

Source: https://www.czarco.com/work/akamai-edge-world-conference-expo

### Akamai - Akamai at GTC 2026
*2026 · GTC · Full exhibit design and build including curved LED and LED Sphere technology with custom applications*

**886k impressions and 59+ PR stories from a single first-time booth debut**

**Challenge.** Akamai was a first-time exhibitor at GTC with no margin for error, needing to make a show-stopping debut that credibly showcased their inference technology partnership with Nvidia rather than simply showing up

**Resolution.** Czarnowski Collective balanced bold creative design using curved LED and LED Sphere technologies with custom applications and strategic messaging that gave Akamai real credibility and a distinctive presence on the floor

**Outcome.** 59+ PR stories, 886k impressions, and 14k engagements from the debut

### RefleXion - RefleXion at ASTRO 2018
*2018 · ASTRO 2018 · Concept design, fabrication, and on-site experience execution for a trade show debut*

**Six tons of revolution, none of the weight**

**Challenge.** The hero product, a six-ton radiotherapy machine, was too heavy to place in the exhibit space, yet the debut required the full visual and experiential impact of the actual machine to convey the magnitude of the technology to a high-stakes healthcare audience

**Resolution.** Used only the machine's outer covers fitted with custom-curved OLEDs over the service hatches to simulate the full look of the machine with high fidelity, and added a proximity-triggered sound system that narrated the experience as attendees approached

**Outcome.** A dramatic commercial debut described as matching the magnitude of the breakthrough, leaving a memorable impression on the ASTRO audience

RefleXion needed to introduce its biology-guided radiotherapy technology at ASTRO 2018, their first commercial showcase, in a way that matched the scale of the breakthrough. The actual machine weighed six tons and could not be placed in the exhibit, so Czarnowski used only its outer covers fitted with custom-curved OLEDs and built a proximity-triggered narration system to simulate the full machine experience in a 30x50 foot space. The result was a debut as dramatic as the science itself, delivering a memorable and high-impact introduction to the ASTRO audience.

Source: https://www.czarco.com/work/reflexion-astro
